**Monday, August 31, 2026 Control-241: swing cron DOW fix deployed and live-verified (HEALTHY code/deploy, DEGRADED external)**

Control-241 at ~08:12 UTC Aug 31 (10:12 +02). Strict GET-only control on 6 endpoints (all 200). DEFECT FOUND: swing cron DOW expression `0 22 * * 1-5` was firing Sunday-Thursday instead of Monday-Friday. Root cause: Cloudflare cron DOW uses 1=Sunday through 7=Saturday (confirmed against official Cloudflare docs), not Unix 0=Sunday. So 1-5 = Sun-Thu. Evidence: swing_cron run 5120 fired Sunday Aug 30 22:00 UTC (status=skipped, market_open=0, safe), run 3182 fired Sunday Aug 23; no swing_cron run on Friday Aug 28 or Friday Aug 21 (both missing from swing_cron trigger filter). The swing strategy itself was not harmed because market_open=0 on Sundays caused a safe skip, but Friday swing runs (the most important weekday for after-close swing evaluation) were being missed entirely. FIX: changed `0 22 * * 1-5` to `0 22 * * 2-6` in wrangler.toml, src/index.ts dispatch match, test/audit-regressions.test.ts (2 assertions), test/runtime-config-schema.test.ts (1 scheduled event). No cap or trading-behavior change. 224 tests / 845 assertions pass, typecheck clean, git diff --check clean. Deployed via direct CF API PUT (HTTP 200, success: true, bundle 328KB, metadata crons confirmed `0 22 * * 2-6`). Live-verified post-deploy: /health 200 v2.6.0, /api/config caps 5000/3700/2000 unchanged, /api/runs shows daytrading cron + reconcile_cron + crypto_cron all dispatching (run 5325 @ 08:11, 5324 @ 08:10, 5323 @ 08:07). Control-217 reliability redeploy holding ~21h, no fifth cron dispatch failure. Run-log contiguous 5226-5325 (100 runs, 0 gaps, 0 errors). Reconcile 3946-5270ms avg 4351ms 30 runs NO creep. 23 swing positions broker-authoritative MV $8,255.70, 0 unattributed. Equity $98,219.02. 3 pending sells 769-771 await Mon open. Crypto fail-closed (MATICUSD empty + AVAXUSD stale ~22h). Fee telemetry stale (Aug 19). Code bd6205e. Next swing_cron dispatch: Monday Aug 31 22:00 UTC (today) — first correct weekday dispatch under the fix. Follow-up: verify swing_cron fires tonight at 22:00 UTC and does NOT fire on Sunday Sep 6.
